{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"#!/bin/bash\\n# H-sweep launch script for context window ablation\\n# Usage: ./launch_hsweep.sh &lt;h_value> &lt;seed>\\n# h_value: 2, 5, 10, 20, full\\n# seed: 1, 2, 3, 4, 5\\n\\nH_VALUE=$1\\nSEED=$2\\n\\nif [ -z \\\"$H_VALUE\\\" ] || [ -z \\\"$SEED\\\" ]; then\\n    echo \\\"Usage: $0 &lt;h_value: 2|5|10|20|full> &lt;seed: 1-5>\\\"\\n    exit 1\\nfi\\n\\n# Validate h_value\\ncase \\\"$H_VALUE\\\" in\\n    2|5|10|20|full) ;;\\n    *) echo \\\"Error: h_value must be one of: 2, 5, 10, 20, full\\\"; exit 1 ;;\\nesac\\n\\n# Validate seed\\nif [ \\\"$SEED\\\" -lt 1 ] || [ \\\"$SEED\\\" -gt 5 ]; then\\n    echo \\\"Error: seed must be 1-5\\\"\\n    exit 1\\nfi\\n\\n# Compute train seed (non-overlapping ranges: seed*10000)\\nTRAIN_SEED=$((SEED * 10000))\\n\\n# Environment setup\\nsource /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh\\nconda activate base\\nexport TMPDIR=/root/autodl-tmp/tmp\\nexport WANDB_DIR=/root/autodl-tmp/wandb\\nexport WANDB_TMPDIR=/root/autodl-tmp/tmp\\n\\ncd /root/cost-verify-agent-rl/RAGEN\\n\\n# Config and experiment name\\nCONFIG=\\\"_alfworld_hsweep_h${H_VALUE}\\\"\\nEXP_NAME=\\\"hsweep-h${H_VALUE}-seed${SEED}\\\"\\n\\n# Log path\\nLOG_PATH=\\\"/root/runs/cost-verify-agent-rl/${EXP_NAME}.log\\\"\\nmkdir -p \\\"$(dirname \\\"$LOG_PATH\\\")\\\"\\n\\necho \\\"=== H-Sweep Launch ===\\\"\\necho \\\"  h_value: $H_VALUE\\\"\\necho \\\"  seed: $SEED (train_seed=$TRAIN_SEED)\\\"\\necho \\\"  config: $CONFIG\\\"\\necho \\\"  experiment: $EXP_NAME\\\"\\necho \\\"  log: $LOG_PATH\\\"\\necho \\\"======================\\\"\\n\\n# Launch with seed and experiment_name override\\npython train.py --config-name \\\"$CONFIG\\\" \\\\\\n    seed.train=$TRAIN_SEED \\\\\\n    trainer.experiment_name=\\\"$EXP_NAME\\\" \\\\\\n    2>&1 | tee \\\"$LOG_PATH\\\"\\n\\n# Auto-chain: trigger next h-sweep run after training completes\\necho \\\"[$(date)] Training complete for h=$H_VALUE seed=$SEED, triggering auto_next...\\\"\\nbash \\\"$(dirname \\\"$0\\\")/auto_next_hsweep.sh\\\" \\\"$H_VALUE\\\" \\\"$SEED\\\"\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.35}}"}

This reasoning process MUST be enclosed within &lt;think> &lt;/think> tags.\\nOnce you've finished your reasoning, you should choose an admissible action for current step and present it within &lt;answer>...&lt;/answer> tags.\\n\", \"parallel_friendly\": false, \"max_actions_per_traj\": 50}, \"SearchQA\": {\"env_type\": \"search\", \"env_config\": {\"max_steps\": 10, \"max_search_results\": 5}, \"max_tokens\": 300, \"max_workers\": 32, \"env_instruction\": \"You are a search agent answering questions by searching for information.\\nUse search[your query] to find relevant documents, and finish[your answer] to submit your final answer.\\n\\nYou should first reason step-by-step about the current situation. This reasoning process MUST be enclosed within &lt;think> &lt;/think> tags.\\nThen provide your action within &lt;answer>...&lt;/answer> tags.\\n\\nExamples:\\n  &lt;think>I need to find information about Ben Platt's father.&lt;/think>&lt;answer>search[Ben Platt father parent]&lt;/answer>\\n  &lt;think>Based on the search results, Ben Platt's father is Henry Platt.&lt;/think>&lt;answer>finish[Henry Platt]&lt;/answer>\\n\", \"parallel_friendly\": true, \"max_actions_per_traj\": 10}, \"Countdown\": {\"env_type\": \"countdown\", \"env_config\": null, \"max_tokens\": 100, \"max_workers\": 32, \"env_instruction\": \"You are solving the Countdown puzzle. Merge equal tiles by sliding Up, Right, Down, or Left.\\nIf a move is invalid (no tiles move), a small penalty is applied. Respond with a single action.\\nExample: &lt;answer>Up&lt;/answer>\\n\", \"max_actions_per_traj\": 700}, \"AlfworldSG\": {\"env_type\": \"alfworld\", \"env_config\": {\"eval_dataset\": \"eval_in_distribution\"}, \"max_tokens\": 512, \"max_workers\": 1, \"env_instruction\": \"You are an expert agent in the ALFRED Embodied Environment.\\nComplete household tasks by navigating and interacting with objects.\\n\\nBefore each action, you MUST first produce a self-guidance assessment in your reasoning:\\n[Assessment: positive/neutral/negative] - evaluate your current progress toward the task goal.\\n[Reasoning: one sentence analyzing what has been accomplished and what remains.]\\n[Suggestion: the best next action from admissible actions.]\\n\\nYour reasoning MUST be enclosed within &lt;think> &lt;/think> tags.\\nAfter the closing &lt;/think> tag, wrap your chosen action in &lt;answer> &lt;/answer> tags.\\nThe action must be a precise ALFWorld command from the admissible actions list (e.g. go to desk 1, pick up pen 2, open drawer 1).\\nDo NOT output natural language descriptions or anything after the &lt;/answer> tag.\\n\\nExample: &lt;think>[Assessment: neutral] I need to find a pen. The desk is a likely location. [Suggestion: go to desk 1]&lt;/think>&lt;answer>go to desk 1&lt;/answer>\\n\", \"parallel_friendly\": false, \"max_actions_per_traj\": 50}, \"BanditTest\": {\"env_type\": \"bandit\", \"env_config\": {\"split\": \"test\"}, \"max_tokens\": 100, \"max_workers\": 32, \"env_instruction\": \"\", \"parallel_friendly\": false, \"max_actions_per_traj\": 1}, \"FrozenLake\": {\"env_type\": \"frozen_lake\", \"env_config\": {\"success_rate\": 0.8}, \"max_tokens\": 100, \"max_workers\": 32, \"env_instruction\": \"You are solving the FrozenLake puzzle. A letter with prime (') means 90° counter-clockwise (e.g., \\\"R'\\\").\\nRespond with a sequence of actions separated by \\\"||\\\".\\nExample: &lt;answer>U&lt;/answer>\\n\", \"max_actions_per_traj\": 20}, \"AlfworldOOD\": {\"env_type\": \"alfworld\", \"env_config\": {\"eval_dataset\": \"eval_out_of_distribution\"}, \"max_tokens\": 512, \"max_workers\": 1, \"env_instruction\": \"You are an expert agent in the ALFRED Embodied Environment.\\nComplete household tasks by navigating and interacting with objects.\\n\\nYou should first reason step-by-step about the current situation. Empty cells are shown as dots (.).\\nPlace numbers one at a time using the format: &lt;answer>place 5 at row 2 col 3&lt;/answer> or &lt;answer>2,3,5&lt;/answer>\\nThe environment will provide feedback on valid/invalid moves and show conflicts if any occur.\\n\", \"parallel_friendly\": false, \"max_actions_per_traj\": 20}, \"AlfworldBIAVR\": {\"env_type\": \"alfworld\", \"env_config\": {\"eval_dataset\": \"eval_in_distribution\"}, \"max_tokens\": 512, \"max_workers\": 1, \"env_instruction\": \"You are an expert agent in the ALFRED Embodied Environment.\\nComplete household tasks by navigating and interacting with objects.\\n\\nAt each step, you have TWO choices:\\n\\n1. **Verify then act**: Output &lt;VERIFY> as the very first token, then produce\\n   a self-guidance assessment before choosing your action. You should only choose action from the available actions list provided later.  Example process: I need a gingko light and 20x20 pillow cover that is hand painted. First search[gingko light 20x20 pillow cover hand painted], answer format: &lt;answer>search[blanket with fleece throw]&lt;/answer>. Valid answer is search[&lt;keywords>] or click[&lt;clickable>].\", \"max_actions_per_traj\": 9}, \"Alfworld\": {\"env_type\": \"alfworld\", \"env_config\": {\"eval_dataset\": \"eval_in_distribution\"}, \"max_tokens\": 512, \"max_workers\": 1, \"env_instruction\": \"You are an expert agent in the ALFRED Embodied Environment.\\nComplete household tasks by navigating and interacting with objects.\\n\\nYou should first reason step-by-step about the current situation. This reasoning process MUST be enclosed within &lt;think> &lt;/think> tags.\\nOnce you've finished your reasoning, you should choose an admissible action for current step and present it within &lt;answer>...&lt;/answer> tags.\\n\", \"parallel_friendly\": false, \"max_actions_per_traj\": 50}, \"SearchQA\": {\"env_type\": \"search\", \"env_config\": {\"max_steps\": 10, \"max_search_results\": 5}, \"max_tokens\": 300, \"max_workers\": 32, \"env_instruction\": \"You are a search agent answering questions by searching for information.\\nUse search[your query] to find relevant documents, and finish[your answer] to submit your final answer.\\n\\nYou should first reason step-by-step about the current situation. This reasoning process MUST be enclosed within &lt;think> &lt;/think> tags.\\nThen provide your action within &lt;answer>...&lt;/answer> tags.\\n\\nExamples:\\n  &lt;think>I need to find information about Ben Platt's father.&lt;/think>&lt;answer>search[Ben Platt father parent]&lt;/answer>\\n  &lt;think>Based on the search results, Ben Platt's father is Henry Platt.&lt;/think>&lt;answer>finish[Henry Platt]&lt;/answer>\\n\", \"parallel_friendly\": true, \"max_actions_per_traj\": 10}, \"Countdown\": {\"env_type\": \"countdown\", \"env_config\": null, \"max_tokens\": 100, \"max_workers\": 32, \"env_instruction\": \"You are solving the Countdown puzzle. You should use the num list to create an equation that equals the target. Example answer format: &lt;think> To find an equation using [3, 5, 2] to get 4. Let's check 2 + 5 = 7, 7 - 3 = 4. So the answer is 2 + 5 - 3 = 4. &lt;/think>&lt;answer>2 + 5 - 3&lt;/answer>\", \"parallel_friendly\": false, \"max_actions_per_traj\": 1}, \"DeepCoder\": {\"env_type\": \"deepcoder\", \"env_config\": {\"max_steps\": 1}, \"max_tokens\": 8000, \"max_workers\": 32, \"env_instruction\": \"You are solving a coding task. Provide a complete Python function solution only. Format: &lt;answer>...&lt;/answer>\", \"parallel_friendly\": true, \"max_actions_per_traj\": 1}, \"game_2048\": {\"env_type\": \"game_2048\", \"env_config\": null, \"max_tokens\": 8192, \"env_instruction\": \"You are playing the 2048 game on a 4x4 grid. Merge equal tiles by sliding Up, Right, Down, or Left.\\nIf a move is invalid (no tiles move), a small penalty is applied. Respond with a single action.\\nExample: &lt;answer>Up&lt;/answer>\\n\", \"max_actions_per_traj\": 700}, \"AlfworldSG\": {\"env_type\": \"alfworld\", \"env_config\": {\"eval_dataset\": \"eval_in_distribution\"}, \"max_tokens\": 512, \"max_workers\": 1, \"env_instruction\": \"You are an expert agent in the ALFRED Embodied Environment.\\nComplete household tasks by navigating and interacting with objects.\\n\\nBefore each action, you MUST first produce a self-guidance assessment in your reasoning:\\n[Assessment: positive/neutral/negative] - evaluate your current progress toward the task goal.\\n[Reasoning: one sentence analyzing what has been accomplished and what remains.]\\n[Suggestion: the best next action from admissible actions.]\\n\\nYour reasoning MUST be enclosed within &lt;think> &lt;/think> tags.\\nAfter the closing &lt;/think> tag, wrap your chosen action in &lt;answer> &lt;/answer> tags.\\nThe action must be a precise ALFWorld command from the admissible actions list (e.g. go to desk 1, pick up pen 2, open drawer 1).\\nDo NOT output natural language descriptions or anything after the &lt;/answer> tag.\\n\\nExample: &lt;think>[Assessment: neutral] I need to find a pen. The desk is a likely location. [Suggestion: go to desk 1]&lt;/think>&lt;answer>go to desk 1&lt;/answer>\\n\", \"parallel_friendly\": false, \"max_actions_per_traj\": 50}, \"BanditTest\": {\"env_type\": \"bandit\", \"env_config\": {\"split\": \"test\"}, \"max_tokens\": 100, \"max_workers\": 32, \"env_instruction\": \"\", \"parallel_friendly\": false, \"max_actions_per_traj\": 1}, \"FrozenLake\": {\"env_type\": \"frozen_lake\", \"env_config\": {\"success_rate\": 0.8}, \"max_tokens\": 100, \"max_workers\": 32, \"env_instruction\": \"You are solving the FrozenLake puzzle. ### 与 8B Cell A 对比

**8B Cell A 参考**：step 1 entropy=0.115(坍缩）→ step 2 entropy=0.605（反弹），ppo_kl=-1.885

**Entropy 反弹模式**：**是，seed1 有更剧烈的版本。**
- Seed1 展现完整的 3 阶段崩溃：
  1. **Entropy 爆炸**（steps 1→5）：0.33 → 1.01，策略扩散到随机
  2. **Entropy 坍缩**（steps 5→13）：1.01 → 0.33，策略重新收缩
  3. **Entropy 反弹**（steps 13→14）：0.33 → 0.55，与 8B 的 0.115→0.605 模式一致
- Seed2 entropy 始终稳定在 0.25-0.43 范围，无异常波动

**Negative KL 模式**：**是，seed1 有 3 个负 KL 步。**
- Step 11: -0.007，step 12: **-0.038**，step 15: -0.010
- 负 KL 出现在 entropy 坍缩阶段（steps 11-12）和反弹后（step 15），与 8B 中反弹伴随负 KL 的模式一致
- Seed2 全程 KL 为正（0.006-0.115），无任何异常

**结论**：sign misattribution 的破坏机制是 **scale-invariant 但 seed-dependent** 的。
- 1.7B seed1 复现了 8B Cell A 的完整 entropy 反弹 + 负 KL 模式，且更剧烈（entropy 从 0.33 爆炸到 1.01 再坍缩回 0.33，伴随 3 次负 KL），最终完全崩溃（success 降到 0%）
- 1.7B seed2 没有出现此模式，entropy 稳定，KL 始终为正，且成功学习（success 上升到 28%）
- 这说明 step-independent h=2 的 sign misattribution 问题跨尺度存在，但触发是随机的——一旦触发，破坏路径相同：entropy 异常振荡 → 负 KL → 完全崩溃
